<content>
1: """Evaluation function compiled from a NEAT genome.
2: 
3: The network maps the canonical 36-feature encoding (see ``ai.features``) to a
4: single tanh output in [-1, 1], interpreted as the expected outcome for the
5: side to move. For search, the value is scaled to ``VALUE_SCALE`` so it lives
6: comfortably below mate scores.
7: 
8: Uses a DAG compiler (FastFeedForwardNetwork) that prunes unreachable and
9: dead-end nodes while preserving neat-python's configured node semantics.
10: A small hash-keyed cache avoids re-evaluating transpositions.
11: """
12: 
13: from __future__ import annotations
14: 
15: from ai.features import encode
16: 
17: VALUE_SCALE = 600.0
18: _CACHE_MAX = 400_000
19: 
20: 
21: class FastFeedForwardNetwork:
22:     """Feed-forward NEAT evaluator with inactive connections pruned.
23: 
24:     Guarantees that:
25:     1. Only nodes reachable from inputs and reaching outputs are evaluated.
26:     2. Topological ordering is strictly respected.
27:     3. Disconnected outputs retain neat-python's initial value of zero.
28:     4. Configured activation and aggregation functions are preserved.
29:     """
30:     __slots__ = ("num_inputs", "output_idx", "eval_steps", "size")
31: 
32:     def __init__(self, num_inputs: int, output_idx: int, eval_steps: list, size: int):
33:         self.num_inputs = num_inputs
34:         self.output_idx = output_idx
35:         self.eval_steps = eval_steps
36:         self.size = size
37: 
38:     @classmethod
39:     def create(cls, genome, config) -> FastFeedForwardNetwork:
40:         input_keys = list(config.genome_config.input_keys)
41:         output_keys = list(config.genome_config.output_keys)
42:         output_key = output_keys[0]
43: 
44:         enabled_conns = {k: cg for k, cg in genome.connections.items() if cg.enabled}
45: 
46:         # 1. Forward reachability from inputs
47:         reachable_from_inputs = set(input_keys)
48:         changed = True
49:         while changed:
50:             changed = False
51:             for (i, o) in enabled_conns:
52:                 if i in reachable_from_inputs and o not in reachable_from_inputs:
53:                     reachable_from_inputs.add(o)
54:                     changed = True
55: 
56:         # 2. Backward reachability to outputs
57:         reaches_outputs = set(output_keys)
58:         changed = True
59:         while changed:
60:             changed = False
61:             for (i, o) in enabled_conns:
62:                 if o in reaches_outputs and i not in reaches_outputs:
63:                     reaches_outputs.add(i)
64:                     changed = True
65: 
66:         # Edges outside an input-to-output path must not block NEAT's scheduler.
67:         active_nodes = reachable_from_inputs & reaches_outputs
68: 
69:         # Build incoming edges for each active node
70:         incoming = {n: [] for n in active_nodes if n not in input_keys}
71:         for (i, o), cg in enabled_conns.items():
72:             if i in active_nodes and o in incoming:
73:                 incoming[o].append((i, cg.weight))
74: 
75:         # Topological sorting
76:         evaluated = set(input_keys)
77:         remaining = set(incoming.keys())
78:         node_order = []
79: 
80:         while remaining:
81:             ready = sorted(n for n in remaining
82:                            if all(i in evaluated for i, _ in incoming[n]))
83:             if not ready:
84:                 cycle_nodes = ", ".join(str(n) for n in sorted(remaining))
85:                 raise ValueError(
86:                     f"Active NEAT phenotype contains a cycle involving nodes: {cycle_nodes}"
87:                 )
88:             for n in ready:
89:                 node_order.append(n)
90:                 evaluated.add(n)
91:                 remaining.remove(n)
92: 
93:         all_nodes = list(input_keys) + node_order
94:         node_to_idx = {k: idx for idx, k in enumerate(all_nodes)}
95:         if output_key not in node_to_idx:
96:             node_to_idx[output_key] = len(all_nodes)
97:             all_nodes.append(output_key)
98: 
99:         eval_steps = []
100:         for n in node_order:
101:             node_data = genome.nodes[n]
102:             act_func = config.genome_config.activation_defs.get(node_data.activation)
103:             agg_func = config.genome_config.aggregation_function_defs.get(node_data.aggregation)
104:             indexed_inputs = tuple((node_to_idx[i], w) for i, w in incoming[n])
105:             eval_steps.append((node_to_idx[n], act_func, agg_func,
106:                                node_data.bias, node_data.response, indexed_inputs))
107: 
108:         out_idx = node_to_idx[output_key]
109:         return cls(len(input_keys), out_idx, eval_steps, len(all_nodes))
110: 
111:     def activate(self, inputs: list[float]) -> list[float]:
112:         if len(inputs) != self.num_inputs:
113:             raise RuntimeError(f"Expected {self.num_inputs:n} inputs, got {len(inputs):n}")
114: 
115:         vals = [0.0] * self.size
116:         vals[:self.num_inputs] = inputs
117:         for target, act_func, agg_func, bias, resp, in_edges in self.eval_steps:
118:             weighted_inputs = [vals[src] * weight for src, weight in in_edges]
119:             vals[target] = act_func(bias + resp * agg_func(weighted_inputs))
120:         return [vals[self.output_idx]]
121: 
122: 
123: class ValueNetwork:
124:     def __init__(self, genome, config):
125:         self.genome = genome
126:         self._net = FastFeedForwardNetwork.create(genome, config)
127:         self._cache: dict[int, float] = {}
128: 
129:     def value(self, pos) -> float:
130:         """Expected outcome in [-1, 1] from the side to move's perspective."""
131:         h = pos.hash
132:         v = self._cache.get(h)
133:         if v is None:
134:             raw = self._net.activate(encode(pos))[0]
135:             v = max(-1.0, min(1.0, raw))
136:             if len(self._cache) >= _CACHE_MAX:
137:                 self._cache.clear()
138:             self._cache[h] = v
139:         return v
140: 
141:     def evaluate(self, pos) -> float:
142:         """Search-scaled evaluation (side-to-move perspective)."""
143:         return self.value(pos) * VALUE_SCALE

(End of file - total 143 lines)
</content>

 """
 
 from __future__ import annotations

 from ai.features import encode
 
 VALUE_SCALE = 600.0
 _CACHE_MAX = 400_000
 
 
+class FastFeedForwardNetwork:
+    """Feed-forward NEAT evaluator with inactive connections pruned.
+
+    Guarantees that:
+    1. Only nodes reachable from inputs and reaching outputs are evaluated.
+    2. Topological ordering is strictly respected.
+    3. Disconnected outputs retain neat-python's initial value of zero.
+    4. Configured activation and aggregation functions are preserved.
+    """
+    __slots__ = ("num_inputs", "output_idx", "eval_steps", "size")
+
+    def __init__(self, num_inputs: int, output_idx: int, eval_steps: list, size: int):
+        self.num_inputs = num_inputs
+        self.output_idx = output_idx
+        self.eval_steps = eval_steps
+        self.size = size
+
+    @classmethod
+    def create(cls, genome, config) -> FastFeedForwardNetwork:
+        input_keys = list(config.genome_config.input_keys)
+        output_keys = list(config.genome_config.output_keys)
+        output_key = output_keys[0]
+
+        enabled_conns = {k: cg for k, cg in genome.connections.items() if cg.enabled}
+
+        # 1. Forward reachability from inputs
+        reachable_from_inputs = set(input_keys)
+        changed = True
+        while changed:
+            changed = False
+            for (i, o) in enabled_conns:
+                if i in reachable_from_inputs and o not in reachable_from_inputs:
+                    reachable_from_inputs.add(o)
+                    changed = True
+
+        # 2. Backward reachability to outputs
+        reaches_outputs = set(output_keys)
+        changed = True
+        while changed:
+            changed = False
+            for (i, o) in enabled_conns:
+                if o in reaches_outputs and i not in reaches_outputs:
+                    reaches_outputs.add(i)
+                    changed = True
+
+        # Edges outside an input-to-output path must not block NEAT's scheduler.
+        active_nodes = reachable_from_inputs & reaches_outputs
+
+        # Build incoming edges for each active node
+        incoming = {n: [] for n in active_nodes if n not in input_keys}
+        for (i, o), cg in enabled_conns.items():
+            if i in active_nodes and o in incoming:
+                incoming[o].append((i, cg.weight))
+
+        # Topological sorting
+        evaluated = set(input_keys)
+        remaining = set(incoming.keys())
+        node_order = []
+
+        while remaining:
+            ready = sorted(n for n in remaining
+                           if all(i in evaluated for i, _ in incoming[n]))
+            if not ready:
+                cycle_nodes = ", ".join(str(n) for n in sorted(remaining))
+                raise ValueError(
+                    f"Active NEAT phenotype contains a cycle involving nodes: {cycle_nodes}"
+                )
+            for n in ready:
+                node_order.append(n)
+                evaluated.add(n)
+                remaining.remove(n)
+
+        all_nodes = list(input_keys) + node_order
+        node_to_idx = {k: idx for idx, k in enumerate(all_nodes)}
+        if output_key not in node_to_idx:
+            node_to_idx[output_key] = len(all_nodes)
+            all_nodes.append(output_key)
+
+        eval_steps = []
+        for n in node_order:
+            node_data = genome.nodes[n]
+            act_func = config.genome_config.activation_defs.get(node_data.activation)
+            agg_func = config.genome_config.aggregation_function_defs.get(node_data.aggregation)
+            indexed_inputs = tuple((node_to_idx[i], w) for i, w in incoming[n])
+            eval_steps.append((node_to_idx[n], act_func, agg_func,
+                               node_data.bias, node_data.response, indexed_inputs))
+
+        out_idx = node_to_idx[output_key]
+        return cls(len(input_keys), out_idx, eval_steps, len(all_nodes))
+
+    def activate(self, inputs: list[float]) -> list[float]:
+        if len(inputs) != self.num_inputs:
+            raise RuntimeError(f"Expected {self.num_inputs:n} inputs, got {len(inputs):n}")
+
+        vals = [0.0] * self.size
+        vals[:self.num_inputs] = inputs
+        for target, act_func, agg_func, bias, resp, in_edges in self.eval_steps:
+            weighted_inputs = [vals[src] * weight for src, weight in in_edges]
+            vals[target] = act_func(bias + resp * agg_func(weighted_inputs))
+        return [vals[self.output_idx]]
+
+

+def _pruned_stock_network(genome, config):
+    """Compile stock NEAT after independently removing inactive connections."""
+    import copy
+    import neat
+
+    connections = [key for key, gene in genome.connections.items() if gene.enabled]
+    reachable = set(config.genome_config.input_keys)
+    changed = True
+    while changed:
+        changed = False
+        for source, target in connections:
+            if source in reachable and target not in reachable:
+                reachable.add(target)
+                changed = True
+
+    required = set(config.genome_config.output_keys)
+    changed = True
+    while changed:
+        changed = False
+        for source, target in connections:
+            if target in required and source not in required:
+                required.add(source)
+                changed = True
+
+    active = reachable & required
+    pruned = copy.deepcopy(genome)
+    for key, gene in pruned.connections.items():
+        if gene.enabled and not (key[0] in active and key[1] in active):
+            gene.enabled = False
+    return neat.nn.FeedForwardNetwork.create(pruned, config)
+
+
+def test_fast_feed_forward_prunes_orphans_and_evaluates_output():
+    import neat
+    import pytest
+    from ai.ladder import load_neat_config
+    from ai.value_net import FastFeedForwardNetwork
+
+    cfg = load_neat_config("neat_value_config.txt")
+    genome = neat.DefaultGenome(1)
+    genome.configure_new(cfg.genome_config)
+
+    for connection in genome.connections.values():
+        connection.enabled = False
+    input_key = cfg.genome_config.input_keys[0]
+    direct = genome.connections[(input_key, 0)]
+    direct.enabled = True
+    direct.weight = 0.75
+
+    output = genome.nodes[0]
+    output.bias = -0.2
+    output.response = 1.7
+
+    # Stock NEAT omits output 0 if this unreachable incoming edge is not pruned.
+    orphan_id = 9999
+    node_gene = neat.genes.DefaultNodeGene(orphan_id)
+    node_gene.bias = 0.5
+    node_gene.response = 1.0
+    node_gene.activation = "tanh"
+    node_gene.aggregation = "sum"
+    genome.nodes[orphan_id] = node_gene
+    # Create connection from orphan to output
+    cg = neat.genes.DefaultConnectionGene((orphan_id, 0))
+    cg.weight = 2.5
+    cg.enabled = True
+    genome.connections[(orphan_id, 0)] = cg
+
+    stock_unpruned = neat.nn.FeedForwardNetwork.create(genome, cfg)
+    assert 0 not in {step[0] for step in stock_unpruned.node_evals}
+
+    reference = _pruned_stock_network(genome, cfg)
+    fast = FastFeedForwardNetwork.create(genome, cfg)
+    inputs = [0.0] * 36
+    inputs[0] = 0.4
+    expected = cfg.genome_config.activation_defs.get(output.activation)(
+        output.bias + output.response * (inputs[0] * direct.weight)
+    )
+
+    assert reference.activate(inputs)[0] == pytest.approx(expected, abs=1e-12)
+    assert fast.activate(inputs)[0] == pytest.approx(expected, abs=1e-12)
+
+
+def test_fast_feed_forward_disconnected_output_matches_stock_zero():
+    import neat
+    from ai.ladder import load_neat_config
+    from ai.value_net import FastFeedForwardNetwork
+
+    cfg = load_neat_config("neat_value_config.txt")
+    genome = neat.DefaultGenome(2)
+    genome.configure_new(cfg.genome_config)
+    for connection in genome.connections.values():
+        connection.enabled = False
+    genome.nodes[0].bias = 0.5
+    genome.nodes[0].response = 2.0
+
+    inputs = [0.25] * 36
+    reference = _pruned_stock_network(genome, cfg)
+    fast = FastFeedForwardNetwork.create(genome, cfg)
+
+    assert reference.activate(inputs) == [0.0]
+    assert fast.activate(inputs) == [0.0]
+
+
+def test_fast_feed_forward_numerical_equivalence_with_stock_neat():
+    """Compare every generated phenotype with stock NEAT after pruning."""
+    import random
+    import neat
+    import pytest
+    from ai.ladder import load_neat_config
+    from ai.value_net import FastFeedForwardNetwork
+
+    cfg = load_neat_config("neat_value_config.txt")
+    random.seed(42)
+
+    # Test across 30 mutated genomes with random hidden nodes and connections
+    for genome_id in range(1, 31):
+        genome = neat.DefaultGenome(genome_id)
+        genome.configure_new(cfg.genome_config)
+
+        # Mutate structure and weights randomly
+        for _ in range(random.randint(1, 5)):
+            genome.mutate_add_node(cfg.genome_config)
+        for _ in range(random.randint(2, 10)):
+            genome.mutate_add_connection(cfg.genome_config)
+        for _ in range(random.randint(3, 15)):
+            genome.mutate(cfg.genome_config)
+
+        stock_net = _pruned_stock_network(genome, cfg)
+        fast_net = FastFeedForwardNetwork.create(genome, cfg)
+
+        for _ in range(5):
+            inp = [random.uniform(-1.0, 1.0) for _ in range(36)]
+            stock_out = stock_net.activate(inp)
+            fast_out = fast_net.activate(inp)
+
+            assert len(fast_out) == len(stock_out) == 1
+            assert fast_out[0] == pytest.approx(stock_out[0], abs=1e-12), \
+                f"Mismatch on genome {genome_id}: fast={fast_out[0]}, stock={stock_out[0]}"
+

+def test_fast_feed_forward_rejects_active_cycle():
+    import neat
+    import pytest
+    from ai.ladder import load_neat_config
+    from ai.value_net import FastFeedForwardNetwork
+
+    cfg = load_neat_config("neat_value_config.txt")
+    genome = neat.DefaultGenome(3)
+    genome.configure_new(cfg.genome_config)
+    for connection in genome.connections.values():
+        connection.enabled = False
+
+    hidden_a, hidden_b = 1001, 1002
+    for node_id in (hidden_a, hidden_b):
+        node = neat.genes.DefaultNodeGene(node_id)
+        node.bias = 0.0
+        node.response = 1.0
+        node.activation = "tanh"
+        node.aggregation = "sum"
+        genome.nodes[node_id] = node
+
+    cycle_edges = [
+        (cfg.genome_config.input_keys[0], hidden_a),
+        (hidden_a, hidden_b),
+        (hidden_b, hidden_a),
+        (hidden_b, 0),
+    ]
+    for key in cycle_edges:
+        connection = neat.genes.DefaultConnectionGene(key)
+        connection.weight = 1.0
+        connection.enabled = True
+        genome.connections[key] = connection
+
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="cycle"):
+        FastFeedForwardNetwork.create(genome, cfg)
+

+def test_arena_opening_plies_execution():
+    from ai.arena import pair_tasks, run_tasks, play_match
+
+    # Verify pair_tasks creates tasks with opening_plies
+    tasks = pair_tasks(("material", 1), ("random",), games=4, base_seed=123, opening_plies=4)
+    assert len(tasks) == 4
+    records = run_tasks(tasks)
+    assert len(records) == 4
+    for r in records:
+        assert r["plies"] >= 4
+        assert "winner" in r
+        assert "p1_strict" in r
+
+    strict, match_records = play_match(("material", 1), ("random",), games=2, base_seed=99, opening_plies=2)
+    assert len(match_records) == 2
+    assert 0.0 <= strict <= 2.0

+def test_deterministic_neat_seeding():
+    import random
+    import neat
+    from ai.ladder import load_neat_config
+
+    cfg = load_neat_config("neat_value_config.txt")
+
+    random.seed(777)
+    pop1 = neat.Population(cfg)
+    g1_keys = sorted(pop1.population.keys())
+    g1_weights = [pop1.population[k].connections[(i, 0)].weight for k in g1_keys for (i, o) in [(i, 0) for i in range(-36, 0)] if (i, 0) in pop1.population[k].connections]
+
+    random.seed(777)
+    pop2 = neat.Population(cfg)
+    g2_keys = sorted(pop2.population.keys())
+    g2_weights = [pop2.population[k].connections[(i, 0)].weight for k in g2_keys for (i, o) in [(i, 0) for i in range(-36, 0)] if (i, 0) in pop2.population[k].connections]
+
+    assert g1_keys == g2_keys
+    assert g1_weights == g2_weights
+

+def test_openings_suite_split_and_validity():
+    from ai.openings import get_validation_openings, get_test_openings
+
+    val_ops = get_validation_openings()
+    test_ops = get_test_openings()
+
+    assert len(val_ops) == 16, f"Expected 16 validation openings, got {len(val_ops)}"
+    assert len(test_ops) == 32, f"Expected 32 test openings, got {len(test_ops)}"
+
+    val_ids = {op.id for op in val_ops}
+    test_ids = {op.id for op in test_ops}
+    assert val_ids.isdisjoint(test_ids), "Validation and test opening IDs must be disjoint"
+
+    val_hashes = {op.position.hash for op in val_ops}
+    test_hashes = {op.position.hash for op in test_ops}
+    assert val_hashes.isdisjoint(test_hashes), "Validation and test positions must be disjoint"
+
+    for op in val_ops + test_ops:
+        assert op.position.turn == 1, "2-ply openings must return turn to player 1"
+        assert len(op.move_history) == 2, "2-ply openings must have 2 moves in history"
+        assert len(op.position.legal_moves()) > 0, "Opening position must have legal moves"
+
+
+def test_pair_opening_tasks_and_raw_logging(tmp_path):
+    import json, os
+    from ai.openings import get_validation_openings
+    from ai.arena import pair_opening_tasks, run_tasks, score_for_a, append_raw_records
+
+    val_ops = get_validation_openings(2)
+    tasks = pair_opening_tasks(("material", 1), ("random",), val_ops, base_seed=42)
+    assert len(tasks) == 4  # 2 openings * 2 colors
+
+    records = run_tasks(tasks)
+    assert len(records) == 4
+    for r in records:
+        assert "opening_id" in r["meta"]
+        assert r["plies"] >= 2
+        assert "winner" in r
+        assert "p1_strict" in r
+
+    strict, shaped = score_for_a(records)
+    assert 0.0 <= strict <= 4.0
+
+    log_file = str(tmp_path / "test_raw.jsonl")
+    append_raw_records(records, log_file)
+    assert os.path.exists(log_file)
+    with open(log_file, "r") as f:
+        lines = [json.loads(line) for line in f if line.strip()]
+    assert len(lines) == 4
+    assert lines[0]["meta"]["opening_id"] == "val_00"

+

+    assert len(tasks1) == len(tasks2) == 2
+    # Opponent spec is identical for both
+    assert tasks1[0][1] == tasks2[0][1] == hof_spec
+    assert tasks1[1][0] == tasks2[1][0] == hof_spec

1: """Path A training: evolve a NEAT value network used inside alpha-beta search.
2: 
3: Design (see README / paper plan):
4: 
5: - ONE population. Each genome is a value network; the playing agent is
6:   alpha-beta search (fixed depth during training) using that network at the
7:   leaves.
8: - Fitness is OUTCOME-ONLY, from color-balanced games against population peers
9:   (random pairings) and against the hall of fame. Draws use a bounded
10:   material tie-break (in (0.4, 0.6)) purely to give early populations a
11:   gradient; wins/losses always dominate.
12: - Hall of fame is Elo-gated: the generation's best genome enters only by
13:   scoring >= 55% in a strict color-balanced gate match against HOF members.
14: - Strength is MEASURED (never trained on) against a fixed ladder of
15:   material-only alpha-beta players, reported as a performance Elo anchored at
16:   random = 0. The ladder is calibrated once and cached.
17: 
18: Everything is seeded and logged to CSV for reproducibility.
19: """
20:

22:

47: # Ladder calibration (once, cached): fixed Elo for the reference opponents.
48: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
49: 
50: def calibrate_ladder(executor, cache_path: str, seed: int = 7,
51:                      games_per_pair: int = 40) -> dict:
52:     if os.path.exists(cache_path):
53:         with open(cache_path) as f:
54:             return json.load(f)
55:     print("Calibrating reference ladder (one-off)...")
56:     rungs = [(n, s) for n, s in LADDER if n in PROBE_RUNGS]
57:     tasks = []
58:     for i, (na, sa) in enumerate(rungs):
59:         for j, (nb, sb) in enumerate(rungs):
60:             if i < j:
61:                 tasks += pair_tasks(sa, sb, games_per_pair,
62:                                     base_seed=seed + 97 * i + j,
63:                                     meta={"pair": (na, nb)})
64:     records = run_tasks(tasks, executor)
65:     games = []
66:     for rec in records:
67:         na, nb = rec["meta"]["pair"]
68:         s_a = rec["p1_strict"] if rec["meta"]["a_is_p1"] else 1.0 - rec["p1_strict"]
69:         games.append((na, nb, s_a))
70:     ratings = fit_elo(games, anchor="random", anchor_rating=0.0)
71:     with open(cache_path, "w") as f:
72:         json.dump(ratings, f, indent=2)
73:     print("Ladder Elo:", {k: round(v) for k, v in ratings.items()})
74:     return ratings
75: 
76: 
77: def performance_rating(results, opp_ratings, lo=-1500.0, hi=4000.0) -> float:
78:     """Rating R such that expected total score vs rated opponents matches the
79:     actual total. results: list of (opp_name, score_total, n_games)."""
80:     actual = sum(s for _, s, _ in results)
81:     n = sum(g for _, _, g in results)
82:     if n == 0:
83:         return 0.0
84:     actual = min(max(actual, 0.25), n - 0.25)  # avoid +/- infinity
85: 
86:     def diff(r):
87:         return sum(expected(r, opp_ratings[o]) * g for o, _, g in results) - actual
88: 
89:     for _ in range(80):
90:         mid = (lo + hi) / 2.0
91:         if diff(mid) < 0:
92:             lo = mid
93:         else:
94:             hi = mid
95:     return (lo + hi) / 2.0
96:

98: import gzip

100: class AtomicCheckpointer(neat.Checkpointer):
101:     """Checkpointer that writes atomically to prevent file corruption on power cut / interrupt."""
102:     def save_checkpoint(self, config, population, species_set, generation):
103:         filename = f"{self.filename_prefix}{generation}"
104:         tmp_filename = f"{filename}.tmp"
105:         with gzip.open(tmp_filename, "w", compresslevel=5) as f:
106:             pickle.dump((generation, config, population, species_set, random.getstate()), f, protocol=pickle.HIGHEST_PROTOCOL)
107:         os.replace(tmp_filename, filename)
108:         print(f"Saved checkpoint to {filename}")

176:     def eval_genomes(genomes, cfg):

209:         records = run_tasks(tasks, executor, chunksize=4)

211:         # ---- aggregate outcome-only fitness ---------------------------------

214:         draws = plies = 0
215:         for rec in records:

217:             shaped_p1 = rec["p1_shaped"]
218:             a_pts = shaped_p1 if m["a_is_p1"] else 1.0 - shaped_p1
219:             points[m["a"]] += a_pts
220:             played[m["a"]] += 1
221:             if m["type"] == "peer":
222:                 points[m["b"]] += 1.0 - a_pts
223:                 played[m["b"]] += 1
224:             draws += 1 if rec["winner"] == 0 else 0
225:             plies += rec["plies"]
226: 
227:         for gid, genome in genomes:

229: 
230:         best_gid = max(gids, key=lambda g: genome_by_id[g].fitness)
231:         best_genome = genome_by_id[best_gid]

233: 
234:         # ---- Elo-gated hall of fame ------------------------------------------

250:                                      max_plies=max_plies, meta={"i": i})
251:             grecords = run_tasks(gtasks, executor, chunksize=2)
252:             strict, _ = score_for_a(grecords)

254:             gate_score = f"{frac:.2f}"

261:                     hall_of_fame.pop(prune_idx)

265:         # ---- save generation best genome --------------------------------------
266:         if best_fit > state["best_ever_fitness"] or state["best_ever"] is None:
267:             state["best_ever"] = candidate
268:             state["best_ever_fitness"] = best_fit
269:         with open(os.path.join(out_dir, "best_value_genome.pkl"), "wb") as f:
270:             pickle.dump(candidate, f)
271:         with open("best_value_genome.pkl", "wb") as f:  # web app default path
272:             pickle.dump(candidate, f)

274:         # ---- periodic strength probe vs fixed ladder ----------------------------

283:                                             rung_specs[rname], val_openings,
284:                                             base_seed=(seed << 8) ^ (gen * 131 + i),
285:                                             max_plies=max_plies,

287:                 precords = run_tasks(ptasks, executor, chunksize=2)
288:                 strict, _ = score_for_a(precords)
289:                 results.append((rname, strict, len(ptasks)))

302:         elapsed = time.time() - t0
303:         n_games = len(records)

<content>
1: """Crash-safe persistence (power-outage-proof file writes).
2: 
3: Every write goes to a temp file in the same directory, is fsync'd, and is
4: then atomically renamed over the target (``os.replace`` is atomic on POSIX
5: and Windows). A power cut can therefore never leave a half-written
6: checkpoint, hall of fame, genome, or calibration file on disk: you keep
7: either the old file or the new one, never a corrupt one.
8: """
9: 
10: from __future__ import annotations
11: 
12: import gzip
13: import json
14: import os
15: import pickle
16: import tempfile
17: 
18: 
19: def _atomic_write(path: str, write_fn) -> None:
20:     dir_ = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(path))
21:     fd, tmp = tempfile.mkstemp(dir=dir_, prefix=".tmp-")
22:     try:
23:         with os.fdopen(fd, "wb") as f:
24:             write_fn(f)
25:             f.flush()
26:             os.fsync(f.fileno())
27:         os.replace(tmp, path)
28:     except BaseException:
29:         try:
30:             os.unlink(tmp)
31:         except OSError:
32:             pass
33:         raise
34: 
35: 
36: def atomic_pickle_dump(obj, path: str) -> None:
37:     _atomic_write(path, lambda f: pickle.dump(obj, f))
38: 
39: 
40: def atomic_pickle_dump_gzip(obj, path: str, compresslevel: int = 5) -> None:
41:     def write(f):
42:         # Closing the GzipFile writes the gzip trailer (end-of-stream
43:         # marker); without this the file is unreadable. fileobj=f stays
44:         # open so _atomic_write can flush+fsync it.
45:         with gzip.GzipFile(fileobj=f, mode="wb",
46:                            compresslevel=compresslevel) as gz:
47:             pickle.dump(obj, gz, protocol=pickle.HIGHEST_PROTOCOL)
48:     _atomic_write(path, write)
49: 
50: 
51: def atomic_json_dump(obj, path: str) -> None:
52:     _atomic_write(path, lambda f: f.write(json.dumps(obj, indent=2).encode()))
53: 
54: 
55: def newest_checkpoint(out_dir: str, prefix: str = "neat-checkpoint-"):
56:     """Path of the highest-numbered checkpoint in out_dir, or None."""
57:     best, best_n = None, -1
58:     if not os.path.isdir(out_dir):
59:         return None
60:     for name in os.listdir(out_dir):
61:         if name.startswith(prefix):
62:             try:
63:                 n = int(name[len(prefix):])
64:             except ValueError:
65:                 continue
66:             if n > best_n:
67:                 best, best_n = os.path.join(out_dir, name), n
68:     return best

(End of file - total 68 lines)
</content>

13104:   - `experiments/final_eval.py` and `experiments/diagnose_ladder.py` call `ai.arena.pair_tasks(..., opening_plies=...)`.
13105:   - Current `ai.arena.pair_tasks()` has no `opening_plies` argument.
13106:   - Those intended evaluations therefore fail before games begin.
13107: - Found a critical reproducibility defect in current training:
13108:   - `ai.train_path_a.run_training()` creates `rng = random.Random(seed)` but does not use it to seed `neat-python`.
13109:   - `neat-python` initialization, mutation, crossover, and reproduction use global `random`.
13110:   - Same `--seed` therefore does not guarantee the same evolutionary run.
13111: - Confirmed resume/provenance anomalies:
13112:   - `runs/pilot_v2_rq1_topo/neat_s0/training_metrics.csv` has duplicate generations `0,1,2`.
13113:   - `runs/rq1_topo/neat_s0/training_metrics.csv` has duplicate generations `169,170,171`.
13114:   - This is consistent with checkpoint generation/resume bookkeeping problems and invalidates naïve aggregation.
13115: - Quantified fitness/Elo misalignment:
13116:   - `runs/path_a/training_metrics.csv`: peak Elo `277` at generation `115`, final recorded Elo `66` at generation `295`; Pearson correlation of probed `best_fitness` with Elo was `-0.093`.
13117:   - `runs/pilot_v2_rq1_topo/neat_s0/training_metrics.csv`: peak Elo `248` at generation `10`, final `31` at generation `295`; correlation `0.049`; mean connections collapsed `36.0 → 4.0`, species increased `1 → 22`.
13118:   - `runs/validation/training_metrics.csv`: Elo `-209 → 62`; correlation with best fitness `-0.838`; mean connections `36.0 → 29.7`, species `1 → 3`.
13119:   - Population mean fitness near `0.5` is expected under symmetric zero-sum peer games and is not evidence of progress.
13120:   - Per-generation best fitness is a noisy order statistic from sparse, changing opponents and is not comparable to independent playing strength.
13121: - Clarified `material-d1/d2/d4 = 0.000`:
13122:   - In all six `runs/validation` probes, the four logged rung scores exactly reconcile with logged `probe_w`, `probe_d`, and `probe_l`.
13123:   - Therefore `0.000` is not merely decimal formatting or final CSV aggregation error in that run; it means the tested champion earned no points in those games.
13124:   - It does not by itself prove the network is intrinsically hopeless: old runs used a broken phenotype path, probes are sparse, all games may share the initial opening, and the material ladder itself is problematic.
13125: - Confirmed ladder instability/non-monotonicity:
13126:   - `runs/path_a/ladder_calibration.json`: d1 `673`, d2 `773`, d4 `584`.
13127:   - `runs/ladder_calibration_g200.json`: d1 `693`, d2 `770`, d4 `544`.

13130:   - `runs/validation/ladder_calibration_v2_g200.json`: d1 `609`, d2 `680`, d4 `720`.
13131:   - Current `ai/ladder.py` still uses `material_eval`; documentation claiming `benchmark_eval` is active is false for this checkout.
13132:   - Random is merely anchored at rating `0`; these ratings are internal performance ratings and are not comparable with human or external Checkers Elo.
13133: - Identified the old phenotype/compiler failure:
13134:   - Stock `neat-python` `feed_forward_layers()` can omit the output when enabled incoming edges originate at unreachable hidden nodes.
13135:   - Checkpoint scan:
13136:     - `runs/path_a/neat-checkpoint-299`: input-dependent `96/96`, stock output scheduled `0/96`; current pruning compiler would recover `96/96`.
13137:     - Pilot checkpoint 299: input-dependent `25/92`, stock scheduled `16/92`, recoverable orphan cases `9/92`, constant networks `67/92`.
13138:     - Validation checkpoint 59: input-dependent `95/95`, stock scheduled `9/95`, recoverable `86/95`.
13139:     - RQ1 checkpoint 229: input-dependent `96/96`, stock scheduled `4/96`, recoverable `92/96`.
13140:   - This is a major implementation-level explanation for old learning failure and topology collapse.
13141: - Found a new unresolved compiler concern:
13142:   - Compared current `FastFeedForwardNetwork` with an independently pruned stock `neat.nn.FeedForwardNetwork`.
13143:   - Maximum absolute output errors were `1.99`, `1.00`, `1.75`, and `1.31` across the four inspected checkpoints.
13144:   - Current compiler therefore cannot yet be accepted as semantically correct; either it or the independent pruning comparison has a defect.
13145:   - `neat.activations.tanh_activation` is standard `math.tanh`, so activation naming alone does not explain the discrepancy.
13146: - Other serious static concerns identified for final audit:
13147:   - `ai.search.Searcher.tt` persists across games and is keyed by board hash while search values depend on repetition history and `hmc`; possible history-dependent TT contamination needs a minimal verification.
13148:   - `ai.arena.play_game()` adjudicates truncations using a three-man material threshold and uses material-shaped draws for fitness, creating objective/search interactions.
13149:   - Current probes use very few games, have no opening randomization, and select a noisy current-generation fitness champion; resulting Elo oscillation is expected.
13150:   - Current HOF gate is small and nonstationary; changing HOF membership changes the training objective.
13151:   - “Same generations/population/games” is not equal compute when search depth or evolved network complexity differs.

13343:     - Stock: `-0.7886630384855012`
13344:     - Current fast formula: `-0.402940104393357`
13345:   - Root cause: `FastFeedForwardNetwork.activate()` hardcodes raw `math.tanh`, while `neat-python`’s configured `tanh_activation` applies NEAT’s scaled tanh input.
13346:   - The custom formula also places `response` around the combined bias/input expression rather than exactly mirroring `activation(bias + response * aggregation(inputs))`; current checkpoints use response `1.0`, so this second issue is latent.
13347:   - Different node order was not the cause; both orders were topologically valid.
13348:   - Therefore the Aug-19 compiler fixes orphan scheduling but does not implement the configured NEAT phenotype semantics.

13352:   - `ai.arena.play_game()` adjudicates truncations by a three-man material threshold.
13353:   - Fitness uses material-shaped draws, while true evaluation uses strict outcomes.
13354:   - HOF membership and peer opponents change each generation, so fitness is nonstationary.
13355:   - Current-generation champion selection is based on sparse noisy fitness games.
13356:   - Same generations/population/games is not equal compute when search depth or network complexity differs.
13357:   - Project delegates mutation, crossover, speciation, stagnation, and reproduction to `neat-python`; it is a standard library implementation but not automatically identical to original canonical NEAT historical-marking semantics.

1: """Uses `pickle` to save and restore populations (and other aspects of the simulation state)."""
2: from __future__ import print_function
3: 
4: import gzip
5: import random
6: import time
7: 
8: try:
9:     import cPickle as pickle # pylint: disable=import-error
10: except ImportError:
11:     import pickle # pylint: disable=import-error
12: 
13: from neat.population import Population
14: from neat.reporting import BaseReporter
15: 
16: 
17: class Checkpointer(BaseReporter):
18:     """
19:     A reporter class that performs checkpointing using `pickle`
20:     to save and restore populations (and other aspects of the simulation state).
21:     """
22:     def __init__(self, generation_interval=100, time_interval_seconds=300,
23:                  filename_prefix='neat-checkpoint-'):
24:         """
25:         Saves the current state (at the end of a generation) every ``generation_interval`` generations or
26:         ``time_interval_seconds``, whichever happens first.
27: 
28:         :param generation_interval: If not None, maximum number of generations between save intervals
29:         :type generation_interval: int or None
30:         :param time_interval_seconds: If not None, maximum number of seconds between checkpoint attempts
31:         :type time_interval_seconds: float or None
32:         :param str filename_prefix: Prefix for the filename (the end will be the generation number)
33:         """
34:         self.generation_interval = generation_interval
35:         self.time_interval_seconds = time_interval_seconds
36:         self.filename_prefix = filename_prefix
37: 
38:         self.current_generation = None
39:         self.last_generation_checkpoint = -1
40:         self.last_time_checkpoint = time.time()
41: 
42:     def start_generation(self, generation):
43:         self.current_generation = generation
44: 
45:     def end_generation(self, config, population, species_set):
46:         checkpoint_due = False
47: 
48:         if self.time_interval_seconds is not None:
49:             dt = time.time() - self.last_time_checkpoint
50:             if dt >= self.time_interval_seconds:
51:                 checkpoint_due = True
52: 
53:         if (checkpoint_due is False) and (self.generation_interval is not None):
54:             dg = self.current_generation - self.last_generation_checkpoint
55:             if dg >= self.generation_interval:
56:                 checkpoint_due = True
57: 
58:         if checkpoint_due:
59:             self.save_checkpoint(config, population, species_set, self.current_generation)
60:             self.last_generation_checkpoint = self.current_generation
61:             self.last_time_checkpoint = time.time()
62: 
63:     def save_checkpoint(self, config, population, species_set, generation):
64:         """ Save the current simulation state. """
65:         filename = '{0}{1}'.format(self.filename_prefix,generation)
66:         print("Saving checkpoint to {0}".format(filename))
67: 
68:         with gzip.open(filename, 'w', compresslevel=5) as f:
69:             data = (generation, config, population, species_set, random.getstate())
70:             pickle.dump(data, f, protocol=pickle.HIGHEST_PROTOCOL)
71: 
72:     @staticmethod
73:     def restore_checkpoint(filename):
74:         """Resumes the simulation from a previous saved point."""
75:         with gzip.open(filename) as f:
76:             generation, config, population, species_set, rndstate = pickle.load(f)
77:             random.setstate(rndstate)
78:             return Population(config, (population, species_set, generation))

1: """Implements the core evolution algorithm."""
2: from __future__ import print_function
3: 
4: from neat.reporting import ReporterSet
5: from neat.math_util import mean
6: from neat.six_util import iteritems, itervalues
7: 
8: 
9: class CompleteExtinctionException(Exception):
10:     pass
11: 
12: 
13: class Population(object):
14:     """
15:     This class implements the core evolution algorithm:
16:         1. Evaluate fitness of all genomes.
17:         2. Check to see if the termination criterion is satisfied; exit if it is.
18:         3. Generate the next generation from the current population.
19:         4. Partition the new generation into species based on genetic similarity.
20:         5. Go to 1.
21:     """
22: 
23:     def __init__(self, config, initial_state=None):
24:         self.reporters = ReporterSet()
25:         self.config = config
26:         stagnation = config.stagnation_type(config.stagnation_config, self.reporters)
27:         self.reproduction = config.reproduction_type(config.reproduction_config,
28:                                                      self.reporters,
29:                                                      stagnation)
30:         if config.fitness_criterion == 'max':
31:             self.fitness_criterion = max
32:         elif config.fitness_criterion == 'min':
33:             self.fitness_criterion = min
34:         elif config.fitness_criterion == 'mean':
35:             self.fitness_criterion = mean
36:         elif not config.no_fitness_termination:
37:             raise RuntimeError(
38:                 "Unexpected fitness_criterion: {0!r}".format(config.fitness_criterion))
39: 
40:         if initial_state is None:
41:             # Create a population from scratch, then partition into species.
42:             self.population = self.reproduction.create_new(config.genome_type,
43:                                                            config.genome_config,
44:                                                            config.pop_size)
45:             self.species = config.species_set_type(config.species_set_config, self.reporters)
46:             self.generation = 0
47:             self.species.speciate(config, self.population, self.generation)
48:         else:
49:             self.population, self.species, self.generation = initial_state
50: 
51:         self.best_genome = None
52: 
53:     def add_reporter(self, reporter):
54:         self.reporters.add(reporter)
55: 
56:     def remove_reporter(self, reporter):
57:         self.reporters.remove(reporter)
58: 
59:     def run(self, fitness_function, n=None):
60:         """
61:         Runs NEAT's genetic algorithm for at most n generations.  If n
62:         is None, run until solution is found or extinction occurs.
63: 
64:         The user-provided fitness_function must take only two arguments:
65:             1. The population as a list of (genome id, genome) tuples.
66:             2. The current configuration object.
67: 
68:         The return value of the fitness function is ignored, but it must assign
69:         a Python float to the `fitness` member of each genome.
70: 
71:         The fitness function is free to maintain external state, perform
72:         evaluations in parallel, etc.
73: 
74:         It is assumed that fitness_function does not modify the list of genomes,
75:         the genomes themselves (apart from updating the fitness member),
76:         or the configuration object.
77:         """
78: 
79:         if self.config.no_fitness_termination and (n is None):
80:             raise RuntimeError("Cannot have no generational limit with no fitness termination")
81: 
82:         k = 0
83:         while n is None or k < n:
84:             k += 1
85: 
86:             self.reporters.start_generation(self.generation)
87: 
88:             # Evaluate all genomes using the user-provided function.
89:             fitness_function(list(iteritems(self.population)), self.config)
90: 
91:             # Gather and report statistics.
92:             best = None
93:             for g in itervalues(self.population):
94:                 if best is None or g.fitness > best.fitness:
95:                     best = g
96:             self.reporters.post_evaluate(self.config, self.population, self.species, best)
97: 
98:             # Track the best genome ever seen.
99:             if self.best_genome is None or best.fitness > self.best_genome.fitness:
100:                 self.best_genome = best
101: 
102:             if not self.config.no_fitness_termination:
103:                 # End if the fitness threshold is reached.
104:                 fv = self.fitness_criterion(g.fitness for g in itervalues(self.population))
105:                 if fv >= self.config.fitness_threshold:
106:                     self.reporters.found_solution(self.config, self.generation, best)
107:                     break
108: 
109:             # Create the next generation from the current generation.
110:             self.population = self.reproduction.reproduce(self.config, self.species,
111:                                                           self.config.pop_size, self.generation)
112: 
113:             # Check for complete extinction.
114:             if not self.species.species:
115:                 self.reporters.complete_extinction()
116: 
117:                 # If requested by the user, create a completely new population,
118:                 # otherwise raise an exception.
119:                 if self.config.reset_on_extinction:
120:                     self.population = self.reproduction.create_new(self.config.genome_type,
121:                                                                    self.config.genome_config,
122:                                                                    self.config.pop_size)
123:                 else:
124:                     raise CompleteExtinctionException()
125: 
126:             # Divide the new population into species.
127:             self.species.speciate(self.config, self.population, self.generation)
128: 
129:             self.reporters.end_generation(self.config, self.population, self.species)
130: 
131:             self.generation += 1
132: 
133:         if self.config.no_fitness_termination:
134:             self.reporters.found_solution(self.config, self.generation, self.best_genome)
135: 
136:         return self.best_genome

1: """Experiment runner for the paper's research questions.
2: 
3: Naming scheme (used in the paper and in run directory names):
4: 
5: - RQ1 "TOPO" (runs/rq1_topo/): Does evolving topology beat fixed-topology
6:   evolution at equal compute?
7:     condition topo-neat   -> neat_value_config.txt  (NEAT, topology evolves)
8:     condition topo-fixed  -> neat_fixed_config.txt  (Blondie24-style control:
9:                                                        weights/biases only)
10:   Both: depth 4 during training, same generations, pop, seeds, compute.
11: 
12: - RQ2 "AMP" (runs/rq2_amp/): How does search depth DURING evolution affect
13:   final strength / sample efficiency? (search-amplified coevolution)
14:     conditions amp-d2, amp-d4, amp-d6 -> neat_value_config.txt, --depth 2/4/6
15:   During-training probes run at the training depth; final comparison is at a
16:   COMMON deep depth via experiments/final_eval.py (default depth 8).
17: 
18: Each condition x seed gets its own out dir, e.g. runs/rq1_topo/neat_s3/.
19: Everything downstream (aggregate.py, final_eval.py) reads this layout.
20: 
21: Usage:
22:   python experiments/run_experiment.py --rq rq1_topo --seeds 0 1 2 3 4 \
23:       --generations 300
24:   python experiments/run_experiment.py --rq rq2_amp --only d6_s2
25: """
26: 
27: from __future__ import annotations
28: 
29: import argparse
30: import os
31: import sys
32: 
33: sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
34: 
35: VALUE_CONFIG = "neat_value_config.txt"
36: FIXED_CONFIG = "neat_fixed_config.txt"
37: FIXED_NONLINEAR_CONFIG = "neat_fixed_nonlinear_config.txt"
38: 
39: CONDITIONS = {
40:     "rq1_topo": {
41:         "neat":            {"config": VALUE_CONFIG, "depth": 4},
42:         "fixed":           {"config": FIXED_CONFIG, "depth": 4},
43:         "fixed_nonlinear": {"config": FIXED_NONLINEAR_CONFIG, "depth": 4},
44:     },
45:     "rq2_amp": {
46:         "d2": {"config": VALUE_CONFIG, "depth": 2},
47:         "d4": {"config": VALUE_CONFIG, "depth": 4},
48:         "d6": {"config": VALUE_CONFIG, "depth": 6},
49:     },
50: }
51: 
52: 
53: def main():
54:     p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
55:     p.add_argument("--rq", choices=sorted(CONDITIONS), required=True)
56:     p.add_argument("--seeds", type=int, nargs="+", default=[0, 1, 2, 3, 4])
57:     p.add_argument("--generations", type=int, default=300)
58:     p.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=None)
59:     p.add_argument("--only", nargs="*", default=None,
60:                    help="run only these <cond>_s<seed> cells, e.g. neat_s0")
61:     p.add_argument("--fresh", action="store_true",
62:                    help="ignore existing checkpoints and start cells over "
63:                         "(default: auto-resume interrupted cells, so after "
64:                         "a power outage you just rerun the same command)")
65:     p.add_argument("--extra", nargs="*", default=[],
66:                    help="extra key=value overrides for run_training "
67:                         "(e.g. peer_rounds=6 probe_games=100)")
68:     args = p.parse_args()
69: 
70:     overrides = {}
71:     for kv in args.extra:
72:         k, v = kv.split("=")
73:         try:
74:             overrides[k] = int(v)
75:         except ValueError:
76:             try:
77:                 overrides[k] = float(v)
78:             except ValueError:
79:                 overrides[k] = v
80: 
81:     from ai.persist import newest_checkpoint
82:     from ai.train_path_a import run_training
83: 
84:     for cond, c in CONDITIONS[args.rq].items():
85:         for seed in args.seeds:
86:             cell = f"{cond}_s{seed}"
87:             if args.only and cell not in args.only:
88:                 continue
89:             out_dir = os.path.join("runs", args.rq, cell)
90:             if args.fresh and os.path.exists(out_dir):
91:                 import shutil
92:                 shutil.rmtree(out_dir)
93:                 print(f"    [fresh] cleared old run directory: {out_dir}", flush=True)
94:             resume = None if args.fresh else newest_checkpoint(out_dir)
95:             print(f"\n=== {args.rq} / {cell} -> {out_dir} ===", flush=True)
96:             if resume:
97:                 print(f"    auto-resuming from {resume}", flush=True)
98:             run_training(config_path=c["config"],
99:                          generations=args.generations,
100:                          depth=c["depth"],
101:                          workers=args.workers,
102:                          seed=seed,
103:                          out_dir=out_dir,
104:                          resume=resume,
105:                          **overrides)
106: 
107: 
108: if __name__ == "__main__":
109:     main()

<type>file</type>
<content>
1: """Seeded, color-balanced match running (serial or across processes).
2: 
3: Every pairing is played an even number of games with colors swapped and
4: per-game seeds derived from a base seed, so experiments are reproducible.
5: 
6: Games truncated at ``max_plies`` are adjudicated: a material edge of at least
7: 3 men decides the game, otherwise it is a draw. Two result scales are
8: reported for player 1:
9: 
10: - ``p1_strict``: 1 / 0.5 / 0   (used for Elo measurement and HOF gating)
11: - ``p1_shaped``: draws become ``0.5 + 0.1*tanh(mat/300)`` (in (0.4, 0.6)),
12:   a bounded tie-breaker that keeps W > any draw > L while giving early,
13:   weak populations a selection gradient. Used for fitness only.
14: """
15: 
16: from __future__ import annotations
17: 
18: import math
19: import random
20: 
21: from checkers.game import CheckersGame
22: from ai.ladder import make_agent
23: 
24: DEFAULT_MAX_PLIES = 150
25: ADJUDICATE_MATERIAL = 300  # centipawns (3 men)
26: 
27: 
28: def play_game(agent_p1, agent_p2, max_plies: int = DEFAULT_MAX_PLIES,
29:               opening_plies: int = 0, opening_seed: int | None = None,
30:               initial_pos=None, initial_moves=None) -> dict:
31:     game = CheckersGame(initial_pos=initial_pos, initial_moves=initial_moves)
32:     plies = len(game.move_history)
33:     if opening_plies > 0:
34:         rng = random.Random(opening_seed)
35:         for _ in range(opening_plies):
36:             if game.is_game_over() or plies >= max_plies:
37:                 break
38:             moves = game.engine_moves()
39:             if not moves:
40:                 break
41:             mv = rng.choice(moves)
42:             game.make_engine_move(mv)
43:             plies += 1
44: 
45:     while plies < max_plies and not game.is_game_over():
46:         agent = agent_p1 if game.current_player == 1 else agent_p2
47:         mv = agent.select(game)
48:         if mv is None:
49:             break
50:         game.make_engine_move(mv)
51:         plies += 1
52: 
53:     winner = game.get_winner()
54:     mat = game.material_diff(1)
55:     reason = game.draw_reason() or ("natural" if winner is not None else "adjudicated")
56:     if winner is None:  # truncated -> adjudicate
57:         if mat >= ADJUDICATE_MATERIAL:
58:             winner = 1
59:         elif mat <= -ADJUDICATE_MATERIAL:
60:             winner = 2
61:         else:
62:             winner = 0
63: 
64:     strict = 1.0 if winner == 1 else (0.0 if winner == 2 else 0.5)
65:     shaped = strict if winner != 0 else 0.5 + 0.1 * math.tanh(mat / 300.0)
66:     return {"winner": winner, "p1_strict": strict, "p1_shaped": shaped,
67:             "plies": plies, "mat": mat, "reason": reason}
68: 
69: 
70: def play_task(task):
71:     """Worker entry point (top level: picklable for ProcessPoolExecutor).
72: 
73:     task can be:
74:     - (spec_p1, spec_p2, seed, max_plies, meta)
75:     - (spec_p1, spec_p2, seed, max_plies, meta, opening_plies)
76:     - (spec_p1, spec_p2, seed, max_plies, meta, opening_plies, initial_pos, initial_moves)
77:     """
78:     spec_p1, spec_p2, seed, max_plies, meta = task[:5]
79:     opening_plies = task[5] if len(task) > 5 else 0
80:     initial_pos = task[6] if len(task) > 6 else None
81:     initial_moves = task[7] if len(task) > 7 else None
82: 
83:     a1 = make_agent(spec_p1, seed=(seed * 2 + 1) & 0xFFFFFFFF)
84:     a2 = make_agent(spec_p2, seed=(seed * 2 + 2) & 0xFFFFFFFF)
85:     rec = play_game(a1, a2, max_plies=max_plies, opening_plies=opening_plies,
86:                     opening_seed=seed, initial_pos=initial_pos, initial_moves=initial_moves)
87:     rec["meta"] = meta
88:     return rec
89: 
90: 
91: def pair_tasks(spec_a, spec_b, games: int, base_seed: int,
92:                max_plies: int = DEFAULT_MAX_PLIES, meta=None,
93:                opening_plies: int = 0):
94:     """Build ``games`` tasks alternating colors (A starts as player 1)."""
95:     tasks = []
96:     for g in range(games):
97:         seed = (base_seed * 1_000_003 + g) & 0x7FFFFFFF
98:         if g % 2 == 0:
99:             tasks.append(((spec_a, spec_b, seed, max_plies,
100:                            {"a_is_p1": True, **(meta or {})}, opening_plies)))
101:         else:
102:             tasks.append(((spec_b, spec_a, seed, max_plies,
103:                            {"a_is_p1": False, **(meta or {})}, opening_plies)))
104:     return tasks
105: 
106: 
107: def pair_opening_tasks(spec_a, spec_b, openings, base_seed: int,
108:                        max_plies: int = DEFAULT_MAX_PLIES, meta=None):
109:     """Build 2*len(openings) tasks: each opening position played twice with colors swapped."""
110:     tasks = []
111:     for i, op in enumerate(openings):
112:         seed = (base_seed * 1_000_003 + i) & 0x7FFFFFFF
113:         op_meta = {"opening_id": op.id, "opening_name": op.name, **(meta or {})}
114:         # Game 1: A is player 1
115:         tasks.append((spec_a, spec_b, seed, max_plies,
116:                       {"a_is_p1": True, **op_meta}, 0, op.position, op.move_history))
117:         # Game 2: B is player 1 (A is player 2)
118:         tasks.append((spec_b, spec_a, seed, max_plies,
119:                       {"a_is_p1": False, **op_meta}, 0, op.position, op.move_history))
120:     return tasks
121: 
122: 
123: def append_raw_records(records: list[dict], filepath: str):
124:     """Append structured game records to a JSONL file."""
125:     import json
126:     import os
127:     os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(filepath)), exist_ok=True)
128:     with open(filepath, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
129:         for r in records:
130:             f.write(json.dumps(r) + "\n")
131: 
132: 
133: def run_tasks(tasks, executor=None, chunksize: int = 1):
134:     if executor is None:
135:         return [play_task(t) for t in tasks]
136:     return list(executor.map(play_task, tasks, chunksize=chunksize))
137: 
138: 
139: def score_for_a(records) -> tuple[float, float]:
140:     """Aggregate (strict, shaped) points for side A over pair_tasks records."""
141:     strict = shaped = 0.0
142:     for rec in records:
143:         if rec["meta"]["a_is_p1"]:
144:             strict += rec["p1_strict"]
145:             shaped += rec["p1_shaped"]
146:         else:
147:             strict += 1.0 - rec["p1_strict"]
148:             shaped += 1.0 - rec["p1_shaped"]
149:     return strict, shaped
150: 
151: 
152: def play_match(spec_a, spec_b, games: int, base_seed: int, executor=None,
153:                max_plies: int = DEFAULT_MAX_PLIES, opening_plies: int = 0):
154:     """Play a color-balanced match; return (strict_points_A, records)."""
155:     tasks = pair_tasks(spec_a, spec_b, games, base_seed, max_plies,
156:                        opening_plies=opening_plies)
157:     records = run_tasks(tasks, executor)
158:     strict, _ = score_for_a(records)
159:     return strict, records

(End of file - total 159 lines)

1: """CLI for the NEAT-checkers project.
2: 
3: Commands:
4:   train   Evolve a NEAT value network (Path A: value net + alpha-beta search).
5:   ladder  Measure a saved genome against the fixed reference ladder (Elo).
6:   play    Play against a trained agent in the terminal.
7:   perft   Validate/benchmark the move generator.
8: """
9: 
10: import argparse
11: import os
12: import pickle
13: import sys
14: import time
15: 
16: DEFAULT_CONFIG = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "neat_value_config.txt")
17: DEFAULT_GENOME = "best_value_genome.pkl"
18: 
19: 
20: def cmd_train(args):
21:     from ai.train_path_a import run_training
22:     run_training(
23:         config_path=args.config,
24:         generations=args.generations,
25:         depth=args.depth,
26:         workers=args.workers,
27:         seed=args.seed,
28:         peer_rounds=args.peer_rounds,
29:         probe_every=args.probe_every,
30:         out_dir=args.out,
31:         pop_size=args.pop,
32:         resume=args.resume,
33:     )
34: 
35: 
36: def cmd_ladder(args):
37:     from concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor
38: 
39:     from ai.arena import pair_tasks, run_tasks, score_for_a
40:     from ai.ladder import LADDER, neat_spec
41:     from ai.train_path_a import PROBE_RUNGS, calibrate_ladder, performance_rating
42: 
43:     with open(args.genome, "rb") as f:
44:         genome = pickle.load(f)
45:     spec = neat_spec(genome, args.config, args.depth)
46:     rungs = [(n, s) for n, s in LADDER if n in PROBE_RUNGS]
47:     if args.d6:
48:         rungs.append(("material-d6", ("material", 6)))
49: 
50:     with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=args.workers) as executor:
51:         cache = os.path.join(args.out, "ladder_calibration.json")
52:         os.makedirs(args.out, exist_ok=True)
53:         ladder_elo = calibrate_ladder(executor, cache, seed=args.seed + 7)
54: 
55:         print(f"\nEvaluating {args.genome} at depth {args.depth} "
56:               f"({args.games} games per rung)...")
57:         results = []
58:         for i, (rname, rspec) in enumerate(rungs):
59:             tasks = pair_tasks(spec, rspec, args.games,
60:                                base_seed=args.seed + 31 * i,
61:                                meta={"rung": rname})
62:             records = run_tasks(tasks, executor)
63:             strict, _ = score_for_a(records)
64:             wins = sum(1 for r in records
65:                        if (r["winner"] == 1) == r["meta"]["a_is_p1"]
66:                        and r["winner"] != 0)
67:             draws = sum(1 for r in records if r["winner"] == 0)
68:             print(f"  vs {rname:12s}: {strict:5.1f}/{len(tasks)} points  "
69:                   f"(W {wins}  D {draws}  L {len(tasks) - wins - draws})")
70:             if rname in ladder_elo:
71:                 results.append((rname, strict, len(tasks)))
72:         elo = performance_rating(results, ladder_elo)
73:         print(f"\nPerformance Elo vs calibrated ladder (random=0): {elo:.0f}")
74:         print("Ladder anchors:",
75:               {k: round(v) for k, v in sorted(ladder_elo.items(),
76:                                               key=lambda kv: kv[1])})
77: 
78: 
79: def cmd_play(args):
80:     from checkers.game import CheckersGame
81:     from ai.ladder import make_agent, neat_spec
82: 
83:     if os.path.exists(args.genome):
84:         with open(args.genome, "rb") as f:
85:             genome = pickle.load(f)
86:         agent = make_agent(neat_spec(genome, args.config, args.depth),
87:                            seed=args.seed)
88:         print(f"Loaded {args.genome} (search depth {args.depth}).")
89:     else:
90:         agent = make_agent(("material", args.depth), seed=args.seed)
91:         print(f"No genome at {args.genome}; playing material-d{args.depth}.")
92: 
93:     game = CheckersGame()
94:     print("\nYou are 'r' (bottom), moving up. Enter moves as: fromRow fromCol toRow toCol")
95:     print("For multi-jumps enter origin and FINAL landing square.\n")
96:     while not game.is_game_over():
97:         print(game.position, "\n")
98:         if game.current_player == 1:
99:             moves = game.get_legal_moves()
100:             for i, m in enumerate(moves):
101:                 caps = f" x{len(m[4])}" if m[4] else ""
102:                 print(f"  [{i}] ({m[0]},{m[1]}) -> ({m[2]},{m[3]}){caps}")
103:             raw = input("Your move (index or 4 numbers, q to quit): ").strip()
104:             if raw.lower() == "q":
105:                 return
106:             try:
107:                 parts = raw.split()
108:                 if len(parts) == 1:
109:                     game.make_move(moves[int(parts[0])])
110:                 else:
111:                     fr_r, fr_c, to_r, to_c = map(int, parts)
112:                     game.make_move((fr_r, fr_c, to_r, to_c, []))
113:             except (ValueError, IndexError) as e:
114:                 print(f"  Invalid move ({e}). Try again.")
115:                 continue
116:         else:
117:             t0 = time.time()
118:             mv = agent.select(game)
119:             game.make_engine_move(mv)
120:             print(f"AI plays {game.move_history[-1].path} ({time.time() - t0:.1f}s)")
121:     print(game.position)
122:     winner = game.get_winner()
123:     msg = {1: "You win!", 2: "AI wins!", 0: f"Draw ({game.draw_reason()})."}
124:     print(msg[winner])
125: 
126: 
127: def cmd_perft(args):
128:     from checkers.bitboard import Position, perft
129:     pos = Position.initial()
130:     for d in range(1, args.depth + 1):
131:         t0 = time.time()
132:         n = perft(pos, d)
133:         dt = time.time() - t0
134:         rate = n / dt if dt > 0 else float("inf")
135:         print(f"perft({d}) = {n:>12,}   {dt:7.2f}s   {rate:,.0f} leaves/s")
136: 
137: 
138: def cli(argv=None):
139:     p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Checkers AI with NEAT + search")
140:     sub = p.add_subparsers(dest="cmd", required=True)
141: 
142:     t = sub.add_parser("train", help="evolve a NEAT value network")
143:     t.add_argument("--generations", type=int, default=200)
144:     t.add_argument("--depth", type=int, default=4,
145:                    help="search depth during training")
146:     t.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=None)
147:     t.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
148:     t.add_argument("--pop", type=int, default=None,
149:                    help="override pop_size from the config")
150:     t.add_argument("--peer-rounds", type=int, default=2)
151:     t.add_argument("--probe-every", type=int, default=5)
152:     t.add_argument("--config", default=DEFAULT_CONFIG)
153:     t.add_argument("--out", default="runs/path_a")
154:     t.add_argument("--resume", default=None,
155:                    help="path to a runs/<name>/neat-checkpoint-N file")
156:     t.set_defaults(fn=cmd_train)
157: 
158:     l = sub.add_parser("ladder", help="measure a genome vs the reference ladder")
159:     l.add_argument("--genome", default=DEFAULT_GENOME)
160:     l.add_argument("--depth", type=int, default=6)
161:     l.add_argument("--games", type=int, default=20)
162:     l.add_argument("--d6", action="store_true", help="also play material-d6")
163:     l.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=None)
164:     l.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
165:     l.add_argument("--config", default=DEFAULT_CONFIG)
166:     l.add_argument("--out", default="runs/path_a")
167:     l.set_defaults(fn=cmd_ladder)
168: 
169:     g = sub.add_parser("play", help="play vs the agent in the terminal")
170:     g.add_argument("--genome", default=DEFAULT_GENOME)
171:     g.add_argument("--depth", type=int, default=6)
172:     g.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=None)
173:     g.add_argument("--config", default=DEFAULT_CONFIG)
174:     g.set_defaults(fn=cmd_play)
175: 
176:     f = sub.add_parser("perft", help="validate/benchmark move generation")
177:     f.add_argument("--depth", type=int, default=7)
178:     f.set_defaults(fn=cmd_perft)
179: 
180:     args = p.parse_args(argv)
181:     args.fn(args)
182: 
183: 
184: if __name__ == "__main__":

<content>
1: """Elo rating estimation from game results.
2: 
3: Simple iterative maximum-likelihood fit of the logistic (Elo) model with
4: draws counted as half a win. Good enough for ladder measurement; anchor the
5: scale by fixing one player's rating (we anchor ``random`` at 0).
6: """
7: 
8: from __future__ import annotations
9: 
10: from collections import defaultdict
11: 
12: 
13: def expected(ra: float, rb: float) -> float:
14:     return 1.0 / (1.0 + 10.0 ** ((rb - ra) / 400.0))
15: 
16: 
17: def fit_elo(games, anchor=None, anchor_rating: float = 0.0,
18:             iterations: int = 400) -> dict:
19:     """games: iterable of (player_a, player_b, score_a) with score in {0, .5, 1}
20:     (or any fraction). Returns {player: rating}."""
21:     games = list(games)
22:     players = sorted({g[0] for g in games} | {g[1] for g in games})
23:     ratings = {p: 0.0 for p in players}
24:     n_games = defaultdict(int)
25:     for a, b, _s in games:
26:         n_games[a] += 1
27:         n_games[b] += 1
28: 
29:     k = 40.0
30:     for it in range(iterations):
31:         grad = defaultdict(float)
32:         for a, b, s in games:
33:             e = expected(ratings[a], ratings[b])
34:             grad[a] += s - e
35:             grad[b] += (1.0 - s) - (1.0 - e)
36:         for p in players:
37:             if n_games[p]:
38:                 ratings[p] += k * grad[p] / n_games[p]
39:         if it and it % 50 == 0:
40:             k = max(k * 0.7, 2.0)
41: 
42:     if anchor is not None and anchor in ratings:
43:         shift = anchor_rating - ratings[anchor]
44:         ratings = {p: r + shift for p, r in ratings.items()}
45:     return ratings

(End of file - total 45 lines)
</content>

The resume defect is confirmed in source: NEAT checkpoints are written after reproduction but store the just-evaluated generation number, so restore reuses that number; HOF, best-genome state, metrics, and RNG are also split across files. I’m replacing that with a versioned atomic checkpoint containing the next generation, global RNG, external training state, manifest, and committed log offsets; legacy/incompatible checkpoints will be rejected rather than silently resumed.

20: class DefaultReproduction(DefaultClassConfig):
21:     """
22:     Implements the default NEAT-python reproduction scheme:
23:     explicit fitness sharing with fixed-time species stagnation.
24:     """
25: 
26:     @classmethod
27:     def parse_config(cls, param_dict):
28:         return DefaultClassConfig(param_dict,
29:                                   [ConfigParameter('elitism', int, 0),
30:                                    ConfigParameter('survival_threshold', float, 0.2),
31:                                    ConfigParameter('min_species_size', int, 2)])
32: 
33:     def __init__(self, config, reporters, stagnation):
34:         # pylint: disable=super-init-not-called
35:         self.reproduction_config = config
36:         self.reporters = reporters
37:         self.genome_indexer = count(1)
38:         self.stagnation = stagnation
39:         self.ancestors = {}
40: 
41:     def create_new(self, genome_type, genome_config, num_genomes):
42:         new_genomes = {}
43:         for i in range(num_genomes):
44:             key = next(self.genome_indexer)
45:             g = genome_type(key)
46:             g.configure_new(genome_config)
47:             new_genomes[key] = g
48:             self.ancestors[key] = tuple()
49: 
50:         return new_genomes
51: 
52:     @staticmethod
53:     def compute_spawn(adjusted_fitness, previous_sizes, pop_size, min_species_size):
54:         """Compute the proper number of offspring per species (proportional to fitness)."""
55:         af_sum = sum(adjusted_fitness)
56: 
57:         spawn_amounts = []
58:         for af, ps in zip(adjusted_fitness, previous_sizes):
59:             if af_sum > 0:
60:                 s = max(min_species_size, af / af_sum * pop_size)
61:             else:
62:                 s = min_species_size
63: 
64:             d = (s - ps) * 0.5
65:             c = int(round(d))
66:             spawn = ps
67:             if abs(c) > 0:
68:                 spawn += c
69:             elif d > 0:
70:                 spawn += 1
71:             elif d < 0:
72:                 spawn -= 1
73: 
74:             spawn_amounts.append(spawn)
75: 
76:         # Normalize the spawn amounts so that the next generation is roughly
77:         # the population size requested by the user.
78:         total_spawn = sum(spawn_amounts)
79:         norm = pop_size / total_spawn
80:         spawn_amounts = [max(min_species_size, int(round(n * norm))) for n in spawn_amounts]
81: 
82:         return spawn_amounts
83: 
84:     def reproduce(self, config, species, pop_size, generation):
85:         """
86:         Handles creation of genomes, either from scratch or by sexual or

88:         """
89:         # TODO: I don't like this modification of the species and stagnation objects,
90:         # because it requires internal knowledge of the objects.
91: 
92:         # Filter out stagnated species, collect the set of non-stagnated
93:         # species members, and compute their average adjusted fitness.
94:         # The average adjusted fitness scheme (normalized to the interval
95:         # [0, 1]) allows the use of negative fitness values without
96:         # interfering with the shared fitness scheme.
97:         all_fitnesses = []
98:         remaining_species = []

100:             if stagnant:
101:                 self.reporters.species_stagnant(stag_sid, stag_s)
102:             else:
103:                 all_fitnesses.extend(m.fitness for m in itervalues(stag_s.members))
104:                 remaining_species.append(stag_s)
105:         # The above comment was not quite what was happening - now getting fitnesses
106:         # only from members of non-stagnated species.
107: 
108:         # No species left.
109:         if not remaining_species:
110:             species.species = {}
111:             return {} # was []
112: 
113:         # Find minimum/maximum fitness across the entire population, for use in
114:         # species adjusted fitness computation.
115:         min_fitness = min(all_fitnesses)
116:         max_fitness = max(all_fitnesses)
117:         # Do not allow the fitness range to be zero, as we divide by it below.
118:         # TODO: The ``1.0`` below is rather arbitrary, and should be configurable.
119:         fitness_range = max(1.0, max_fitness - min_fitness)
120:         for afs in remaining_species:

122:             msf = mean([m.fitness for m in itervalues(afs.members)])
123:             af = (msf - min_fitness) / fitness_range

125: 
126:         adjusted_fitnesses = [s.adjusted_fitness for s in remaining_species]
127:         avg_adjusted_fitness = mean(adjusted_fitnesses) # type: float
128:         self.reporters.info("Average adjusted fitness: {:.3f}".format(avg_adjusted_fitness))
129: 
130:         # Compute the number of new members for each species in the new generation.
131:         previous_sizes = [len(s.members) for s in remaining_species]

180:                 # genetically identical clone of the parent (but with a different ID).
181:                 gid = next(self.genome_indexer)
182:                 child = config.genome_type(gid)
183:                 child.configure_crossover(parent1, parent2, config.genome_config)
184:                 child.mutate(config.genome_config)
185:                 new_population[gid] = child
186:                 self.ancestors[gid] = (parent1_id, parent2_id)

The first falsification test exposed an additional stock-NEAT resume hazard: reporter closures and `itertools.count` ID generators leak into serialized state. I’m storing the generator positions explicitly and rebinding reporters on restore, which is required for exact continuation rather than merely making the file load.

_Thinking:_

**Identifying reproducibility issues**

I realize that `load_neat_config` caches and returns the same mutable config, which can lead to a reproducibility bug because the node indexer and population size persist across runs. This affects `run_training`, as it uses this cached config while workers create agents and read the ValueNetwork, causing non-determinism. The tests only check weights and not nodes. I need to fix `load_neat_config` to either return a fresh config or ensure it’s cached immutably. A fresh config would help training and testing.**Updating config loading and testing**

I’m considering adding a `fresh` parameter to the `load_neat_config` function, allowing for a fresh configuration when specified. The training calls should also use this fresh config to maintain determinism. The existing tests for deterministic seeding use the same configuration but need better handling for mutable aspects, like the node indexer. I see a need for separate tests with fresh configs, and I can use a helper to avoid warnings during counter resets, ensuring no mutation of the original counters. This approach keeps everything tidy while preserving functionality!

The remaining divergence is now isolated: `load_neat_config()` returns one mutable cached config, so NEAT’s node-ID counter leaks across supposedly fresh runs. Training must receive a fresh config; worker-side read-only phenotype loading can remain cached for performance.

**Output:**
